- Minutes lost × hourly wage × labor burden × employees × work days. The labor burden multiplier captures the fact that you're not just paying the wage — you're paying employer FICA, workers comp, benefits, and PTO accrual on that wage too. Default 1.32x is the U.S. small business average.

### Atlas One Calculators (53 tools).html L4139 [text]
- In-house cost = HR/admin staff time + software + benefits-admin time + compliance/risk buffer — the functions a PEO absorbs. PEO cost = PEPM × employees × 12, or payroll × fee%. Break-even PEO fee is the monthly per-employee price at which the PEO exactly matches what you spend today, including any purchasing-power savings you've modeled.
+ In-house cost = HR/admin staff time + software + benefits-admin time + compliance/risk buffer, the functions a PEO absorbs. PEO cost = PEPM × employees × 12, or payroll × fee%. Break-even PEO fee is the monthly per-employee price at which the PEO exactly matches what you spend today, including any purchasing-power savings you've modeled.

This estimates your back-tax exposure so you can act before an auditor does.
+ decision, you can owe sales tax in a state you've never set foot in, just by selling enough into it. - This is a planning estimate, not a determination. Actual nexus depends on each state's specific dollar/transaction thresholds, marketplace-facilitator rules (Amazon/eBay/Etsy may already collect for you), product taxability, and physical-presence factors (inventory in a warehouse, remote employees, affiliates). Economic-nexus thresholds are typically measured on the prior or current calendar year.
+ This is a planning estimate, not a determination. Actual nexus depends on each state's specific dollar/transaction thresholds, marketplace-facilitator rules (Amazon/eBay/Etsy may already collect for you), product taxability, and physical-presence factors (inventory in a warehouse, remote employees, affiliates). Economic-nexus thresholds are typically measured on the prior or current calendar year.
### Atlas One Calculators (53 tools).html L4218 [text]
- A wrong hire costs far more than the salary you paid them. Between recruiting, onboarding, lost productivity, management drag, severance, and doing it all again, the U.S. Department of Labor pegs the minimum at ~30% of the employee's first-year earnings — and for many roles it runs 1.5×–3× salary. This puts a real number on your last one.
+ A wrong hire costs far more than the salary you paid them. Between recruiting, onboarding, lost productivity, management drag, severance, and doing it all again, the U.S. Department of Labor pegs the minimum at ~30% of the employee's first-year earnings, and for many roles it runs 1.5× to 3× salary. This puts a real number on your last one.

- <p>The Company works to reduce ergonomic risk factors — such as repetitive motion, awkward postures, and manual lifting — through workplace design, safe lifting practices, and employee training. Employees are encouraged to report discomfort early.</p>

### Safety Manual Builder (Bilingual OSHA).html L181 [js]
- <p>La Compañía trabaja para reducir los factores de riesgo ergonómico —como el movimiento repetitivo, las posturas incómodas y el levantamiento manual— mediante el diseño del lugar de trabajo, prácticas de levantamiento seguro y capacitación de los empleados. Se anima a los empleados a informar cualquier molestia de manera temprana.</p>
+ <p>La Compañía trabaja para reducir los factores de riesgo ergonómico (como el movimiento repetitivo, las posturas incómodas y el levantamiento manual) mediante el diseño del lugar de trabajo, prácticas de levantamiento seguro y capacitación de los empleados.
